Returns duplicate flag. Inserts row if not duplicate and retention_days > 0\n    if retention_days <= 0:\n        return False\n    now = datetime.now()\n    cutoff = now - timedelta(days=retention_days)\n\n    conn = None\n    try:\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(index_db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\n            """\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS file_index (\n              id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n              file_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n              filename TEXT NOT NULL,\n              timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,\n              record_count INTEGER,\n              UNIQUE(file_hash, timestamp)\n            )\n            """\n        )\n        # Fetch rows with same file_hash\n        cur.execute("SELECT file_hash, filename, timestamp FROM file_index WHERE file_hash = ?", (file_hash,))\n        rows = cur.fetchall()\n        duplicate = False\n        for _, _, ts in rows:\n            try:\n                ts_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)\n            except Exception:\n                continue\n            if ts_dt >= cutoff:\n                duplicate = True\n                break\n        if not duplicate:\n            # 12:28:33  Sending keys: ['cat > validate_eft.py << \'EOFPYTHON\'\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n"""EFT Payment File Validator."""\n\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport hashlib\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nfrom decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple\n\n\nclass EFTValidator:\n    def __init__(self, schema_path: str, index_db: str, clearing_accounts_path: str, retention_days: int = 5, payees_db_path: str = None):\n        self.schema_path = schema_path\n        self.db_path = index_db\n        self.retention_days = retention_days  # Use the actual parameter\n        self.payees_db_path = payees_db_path\n\n        with open(schema_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:\n            self.schema = json.load(f)\n\n        self.clearing_accounts: set[str] = set()\n        if Path(clearing_accounts_path).exists():\n            with open(clearing_accounts_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:\n                self.clearing_accounts = {line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()}\n\n        self._init_database()\n\n    def _init_database(self) -> None:\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\n            """\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS file_index (\n                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                file_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n                filename TEXT NOT NULL,\n                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,\n                record_count INTEGER,\n                UNIQUE(file_hash, timestamp)\n            )\n            """\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        conn.close()\n\n    def _compute_hash(self, content: str) -> str:\n        """Compute SHA-256 hash of canonicalized content."""\n        # Step 1-2: Normalize line endings\n        normalized = content.replace("\\r\\n", "\\n").replace("\\r", "\\n")\n        \n        # Step 3: Split into lines\n        lines = normalized.split("\\n")\n        \n        # Step 4: Remove trailing spaces and tabs from each line\n        lines = [line.rstrip(" \\t") for line in lines]\n        \n        # Step 5: Remove trailing empty lines\n        while lines and lines[-1] == "":\n            lines.pop()\n        \n        # Step 6: Join with \\n and append final \\n\n        if lines:\n            canonical = "\\n".join(lines) + "\\n"\n        else:\n            canonical = ""\n        \n        # Step 7: Hash with SHA-256\n        return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()\n\n    def check_duplicate(self, file_hash: str) -> bool:\n        """Check if file_hash exists within retention window."""\n        if self.retention_days <= 0:\n            return False\n        \n        cutoff = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=self.retention_days)\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\n            "SELECT timestamp FROM file_index WHERE file_hash = ?",\n            (file_hash,),\n        )\n        rows = cur.fetchall()\n        conn.close()\n        \n        for (ts_str,) in rows:\n            try:\n                ts = datetime.fromisoformat(ts_str)\n                if ts >= cutoff:\n                    return True\n            except (ValueError, AttributeError):\n                # Ignore rows with unparseable timestamps\n                continue\n        \n        return False\n\n    def record_file(self, file_hash: str, filename: str, record_count: int) -> None:\n        """Record file submission in index database."""\n        if self.retention_days <= 0:\n            return  # Don\'t record when retention is disabled\n        \n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        try:\n            cur.execute(\n                "INSERT INTO file_index (file_hash, filename, timestamp, record_count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",\n                (file_hash, filename, datetime.now().isoformat(), record_count),\n            )\n            conn.commit()\n        finally:\n            conn.close()\n\n    def validate_record_length(self, line: str, line_num: int, record_length: int) -> Tuple[str, List[str]]:\n        """Validate and normalize record length."""\n        errors = []\n        \n        if len(line) < record_length:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Record too short (expected {record_length}, got {len(line)})")\n            return line, errors\n        elif len(line) == record_length:\n            return line, errors\n        else:  # len(line) > record_length\n            suffix = line[record_length:]\n            if suffix.strip() == "":\n                # Only whitespace padding, accept and truncate\n                return line[:record_length], errors\n            else:\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Record too long with non-whitespace content")\n                return line, errors\n        \n    def parse_and_validate_record(self, line: str, line_num: int) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], List[str]]:\n        """Parse and validate a single record."""\n        errors: List[str] = []\n        record: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        \n        record_length = self.schema["record_length"]\n        \n        # Validate record length\n        line, length_errors = self.validate_record_length(line, line_num, record_length)\n        errors.extend(length_errors)\n        \n        if length_errors:\n            # Can\'t parse fields if length is wrong\n            return record, errors\n        \n        # Parse fields\n        for field in self.schema["fields"]:\n            name = field["name"]\n            start = int(field["start"])\n            length = int(field["length"])\n            required = bool(field.get("required", False))\n            ftype = field.get("type", "string")\n            \n            raw = line[start : start + length]\n            value = raw.strip()\n            record[name] = value\n            \n            # Required field check\n            if required and not value:\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is required")\n                continue\n            \n            if not value:\n                continue\n            \n            # Type-specific validation\n            if ftype == "string":\n                pattern = field.get("pattern")\n                if pattern and not re.fullmatch(pattern, value):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} does not match pattern")\n            \n            elif ftype == "decimal":\n                try:\n                    amt = Decimal(value)\n                    if amt <= 0:\n                        errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} must be > 0")\n                    # Check decimal places\n                    if \'.\' in value:\n                        decimal_part = value.split(\'.\')[1]\n                        if len(decimal_part) > 2:\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} has more than 2 decimal places")\n                except (InvalidOperation, ValueError):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is not a valid decimal")\n            \n            elif ftype == "date":\n                date_format = field.get("format", "%Y-%m-%d")\n                try:\n                    datetime.strptime(value, date_format)\n                except ValueError:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is not a valid date")\n        \n        # Field-specific validations\n        field_errors = self.validate_field_specific(record, line_num)\n        errors.extend(field_errors)\n        \n        return record, errors\n\n    def validate_field_specific(self, record: Dict[str, Any], line_num: int) -> List[str]:\n        """Perform field-specific validations."""\n        errors = []\n        \n        # eftno validation\n        if "eftno" in record:\n            eftno = record["eftno"]\n            if eftno:  # Only validate if not empty\n                if not re.match(r"^[A-Za-z0-9]+$", eftno):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: eftno must be alphanumeric")\n        \n        # bank_code validation\n        if "bank_code" in record:\n            bank_code = record["bank_code"]\n            if bank_code:  # Only validate if not empty\n                if not bank_code[0].isdigit():\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: bank_code must start with a digit")\n                if not re.match(r"^[A-Z0-9]+$", bank_code):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: bank_code must contain only uppercase letters and digits")\n        \n        # account_no validation\n        if "account_no" in record:\n            acct = record["account_no"]\n            if acct:  # Only validate if not empty\n                # Must be all digits\n                if not re.match(r"^\\d+$", acct):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: account_no must be all digits")\n                # Length 8-20\n                elif len(acct) < 8 or len(acct) > 20:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: account_no must be 8-20 digits")\n                else:\n                    # Forbidden prefixes\n                    if acct.startswith(("0000", "0001", "0010", "0100")):\n                        errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: account_no has forbidden prefix")\n                    # First 4 digits must not be only 0 and 1\n                    if len(acct) >= 4:\n                        first_four = acct[:4]\n                        if set(first_four) <= {\'0\', \'1\'}:\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: First 4 digits of account_no cannot consist solely of 0 and 1")\n                    # Last 4 digits must not contain 0\n                    if len(acct) >= 4:\n                        last_four = acct[-4:]\n                        if \'0\' in last_four:\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Last 4 digits of account_no cannot contain 0")\n        \n        # amount validation (additional to decimal type validation)\n        if "amount" in record:\n            amt_str = record["amount"]\n            if amt_str:  # Only validate if not empty\n                try:\n                    amt = Decimal(amt_str)\n                    if amt <= 0:\n                        errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: amount must be > 0")\n                except (InvalidOperation, ValueError):\n                    pass  # Already reported by type validation\n        \n        # clearing_account validation\n        if "clearing_account" in record:\n            clearing = record["clearing_account"]\n            if clearing:  # Only validate if not empty\n                if clearing not in self.clearing_accounts:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Invalid clearing account")\n        \n        # Payees database validation\n        if self.payees_db_path and Path(self.payees_db_path).exists():\n            payee_errors = self.validate_payee(record, line_num)\n            errors.extend(payee_errors)\n        \n        return errors\n\n    def validate_payee(self, record: Dict[str, Any], line_num: int) -> List[str]:\n        """Validate against payees database."""\n        errors = []\n        \n        account_no = record.get("account_no", "")\n        if not account_no:\n            return errors\n        \n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.payees_db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute("SELECT payee_name, fraud_flag FROM payees WHERE account_no = ?", (account_no,))\n        row = cur.fetchone()\n        conn.close()\n        \n        if not row:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Account {account_no} not found in payee database")\n            return errors\n        \n        db_name, fraud_flag = row\n        \n        # Check fraud flag\n        if fraud_flag == 1:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Account {account_no} is flagged for fraud risk")\n        \n        # Check payee name match (case-insensitive, whitespace normalized)\n        file_payee_name = record.get("payee_name", "")\n        if file_payee_name:\n            # Normalize: trim and collapse whitespace\n            def normalize_name(name: str) -> str:\n                # Strip leading/trailing whitespace\n                name = name.strip()\n                # Collapse internal whitespace to single space\n                name = re.sub(r\'\\s+\', \' \', name)\n                # Case-insensitive comparison with casefold\n                return name.casefold()\n            \n            if normalize_name(file_payee_name) != normalize_name(db_name):\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Payee name mismatch for account {account_no}")\n        \n        return errors\n\n    def validate_file(self, file_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        """Validate an EFT payment file."""\n        # Read file content\n        content = Path(file_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")\n        \n        # Compute file hash\n        file_hash = self._compute_hash(content)\n        \n        # Check for duplicate\n        is_dup = self.check_duplicate(file_hash)\n        \n        # Parse records\n        normalized = content.replace("\\r\\n", "\\n").replace("\\r", "\\n")\n        lines = normalized.split("\\n")\n        \n        # Remove trailing empty lines only\n        while lines and lines[-1] == "":\n            lines.pop()\n        \n        records_processed = len(lines)\n        \n        errors: List[str] = []\n        warnings: List[str] = []\n        \n        # Validate each record\n        for i, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):\n            record, rec_errors = self.parse_and_validate_record(line, i)\n            errors.extend(rec_errors)\n        \n        # Record file if not duplicate\n        if not is_dup:\n            filename = Path(file_path).name\n            self.record_file(file_hash, filename, records_processed)\n        \n        return {\n            "duplicate": is_dup,\n            "n_errors": len(errors),\n            "n_warnings": len(warnings),\n            "errors": errors,\n            "warnings": warnings,\n            "file_hash": file_hash,\n            "records_processed": records_processed,\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    import argparse\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\'Validate EFT payment files\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--file\', required=True, help=\'Path to EFT file to validate\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--schema\', required=True, help=\'Path to schema JSON\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--index\', required=True, help=\'Path to SQLite index database\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--clearing-accounts\', required=True, help=\'Path to clearing accounts file\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--retention-days\', type=int, default=5, help=\'Duplicate detection retention window (days)\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--payees-db\', help=\'Path to payees database\')\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    try:\n        if not Path(args.file).exists():\n            # File not found - still output JSON\n            report = {\n                "duplicate": False,\n                "n_errors": 1,\n                "n_warnings": 0,\n                "errors": [f"File not found: {args.file}"],\n                "warnings": [],\n                "file_hash": "",\n                "records_processed": 0,\n            }\n            print(json.dumps(report))\n            sys.exit(1)\n\n        validator = EFTValidator(\n            schema_path=args.schema,\n            index_db=args.index,\n            clearing_accounts_path=args.clearing_accounts,\n            retention_days=args.retention_days,\n            payees_db_path=args.payees_db\n        )\n\n        report = validator.validate_file(args.file)\n\n        print(json.dumps(report))\n\n        # Exit code: 0 if valid and not duplicate, 1 otherwise\n        if report[\'duplicate\'] or report[\'n_errors\'] > 0:\n            sys.exit(1)\n        else:\n            sys.exit(0)\n    \n    except Exception as e:\n        # Avoid Python traceback - output JSON error\n        report = {\n            "duplicate": False,\n            "n_errors": 1,\n            "n_warnings": 0,\n            "errors": [f"Validation error: {str(e)}"],\n            "warnings": [],\n            "file_hash": "",\n            "records_processed": 0,\n        }\n        print(json.dumps(report))\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n\nif __name__ == \'__main__\':\n    main()\nEOFPYTHON\n'] min_timeout_sec: 0.5 max_timeout_sec: 180.0

12:28:33  Sending keys: ['cat > validate_eft.py << \'EOFPYTHON\'\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n"""EFT Payment File Validator."""\n\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport hashlib\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nfrom decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple\n\n\nclass EFTValidator:\n    def __init__(self, schema_path: str, index_db: str, clearing_accounts_path: str, retention_days: int = 5, payees_db_path: str = None):\n        self.schema_path = schema_path\n        self.db_path = index_db\n        self.retention_days = retention_days\n        self.payees_db_path = payees_db_path\n\n        with open(schema_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:\n            self.schema = json.load(f)\n\n        self.clearing_accounts: set[str] = set()\n        if Path(clearing_accounts_path).exists():\n            with open(clearing_accounts_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:\n                self.clearing_accounts = {line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()}\n\n        self._init_database()\n\n    def _init_database(self) -> None:\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\n            """\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS file_index (\n                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                file_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n                filename TEXT NOT NULL,\n                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,\n                record_count INTEGER,\n                UNIQUE(file_hash, timestamp)\n            )\n            """\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        conn.close()\n\n    def _compute_hash(self, content: str) -> str:\n        """Compute SHA-256 hash of canonicalized content."""\n        normalized = content.replace("\\r\\n", "\\n").replace("\\r", "\\n")\n        lines = normalized.split("\\n")\n        lines = [line.rstrip(" \\t") for line in lines]\n        while lines and lines[-1] == "":\n            lines.pop()\n        if lines:\n            canonical = "\\n".join(lines) + "\\n"\n        else:\n            canonical = ""\n        return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()\n\n    def check_duplicate(self, file_hash: str) -> bool:\n        """Check if file_hash exists within retention window."""\n        if self.retention_days <= 0:\n            return False\n        \n        cutoff = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=self.retention_days)\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\n            "SELECT timestamp FROM file_index WHERE file_hash = ?",\n            (file_hash,),\n        )\n        rows = cur.fetchall()\n        conn.close()\n        \n        for (ts_str,) in rows:\n            try:\n                ts = datetime.fromisoformat(ts_str)\n                if ts >= cutoff:\n                    return True\n            except (ValueError, AttributeError):\n                continue\n        \n        return False\n\n    def record_file(self, file_hash: str, filename: str, record_count: int) -> None:\n        """Record file submission in index database."""\n        if self.retention_days <= 0:\n            return\n        \n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        try:\n            cur.execute(\n                "INSERT INTO file_index (file_hash, filename, timestamp, record_count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",\n                (file_hash, filename, datetime.now().isoformat(), record_count),\n            )\n            conn.commit()\n        finally:\n            conn.close()\n\n    def validate_record_length(self, line: str, line_num: int, record_length: int) -> Tuple[str, List[str]]:\n        """Validate and normalize record length."""\n        errors = []\n        \n        if len(line) < record_length:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Record too short (expected {record_length}, got {len(line)})")\n            return line, errors\n        elif len(line) == record_length:\n            return line, errors\n        else:\n            suffix = line[record_length:]\n            if suffix.strip() == "":\n                return line[:record_length], errors\n            else:\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Record too long with non-whitespace content")\n                return line, errors\n        \n    def parse_and_validate_record(self, line: str, line_num: int) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], List[str]]:\n        """Parse and validate a single record."""\n        errors: List[str] = []\n        record: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        \n        record_length = self.schema["record_length"]\n        \n        line, length_errors = self.validate_record_length(line, line_num, record_length)\n        errors.extend(length_errors)\n        \n        if length_errors:\n            return record, errors\n        \n        for field in self.schema["fields"]:\n            name = field["name"]\n            start = int(field["start"])\n            length = int(field["length"])\n            required = bool(field.get("required", False))\n            ftype = field.get("type", "string")\n            \n            raw = line[start : start + length]\n            value = raw.strip()\n            record[name] = value\n            \n            if required and not value:\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is required")\n                continue\n            \n            if not value:\n                continue\n            \n            if ftype == "string":\n                pattern = field.get("pattern")\n                if pattern and not re.fullmatch(pattern, value):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} does not match pattern")\n            \n            elif ftype == "decimal":\n                try:\n                    amt = Decimal(value)\n                    if amt <= 0:\n                        errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} must be > 0")\n                    if \'.\' in value:\n                        decimal_part = value.split(\'.\')[1]\n                        if len(decimal_part) > 2:\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} has more than 2 decimal places")\n                except (InvalidOperation, ValueError):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is not a valid decimal")\n            \n            elif ftype == "date":\n                date_format = field.get("format", "%Y-%m-%d")\n                try:\n                    datetime.strptime(value, date_format)\n                except ValueError:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is not a valid date")\n        \n        field_errors = self.validate_field_specific(record, line_num)\n        errors.extend(field_errors)\n        \n        return record, errors\n\n    def validate_field_specific(self, record: Dict[str, Any], line_num: int) -> List[str]:\n        """Perform field-specific validations."""\n        errors = []\n        \n        if "eftno" in record:\n            eftno = record["eftno"]\n            if eftno:\n                if not re.match(r"^[A-Za-z0-9]+$", eftno):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: eftno must be alphanumeric")\n        \n        if "bank_code" in record:\n            bank_code = record["bank_code"]\n            if bank_code:\n                if not bank_code[0].isdigit():\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: bank_code must start with a digit")\n                if not re.match(r"^[A-Z0-9]+$", bank_code):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: bank_code must contain only uppercase letters and digits")\n        \n        if "account_no" in record:\n            acct = record["account_no"]\n            if acct:\n                if not re.match(r"^\\d+$", acct):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: account_no must be all digits")\n                elif len(acct) < 8 or len(acct) > 20:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: account_no must be 8-20 digits")\n                else:\n                    if acct.startswith(("0000", "0001", "0010", "0100")):\n                        errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: account_no has forbidden prefix")\n                    if len(acct) >= 4:\n                        first_four = acct[:4]\n                        if set(first_four) <= {\'0\', \'1\'}:\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: First 4 digits of account_no cannot consist solely of 0 and 1")\n                    if len(acct) >= 4:\n                        last_four = acct[-4:]\n                        if \'0\' in last_four:\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Last 4 digits of account_no cannot contain 0")\n        \n        if "clearing_account" in record:\n            clearing = record["clearing_account"]\n            if clearing:\n                if clearing not in self.clearing_accounts:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Invalid clearing account")\n        \n        if self.payees_db_path and Path(self.payees_db_path).exists():\n            payee_errors = self.validate_payee(record, line_num)\n            errors.extend(payee_errors)\n        \n        return errors\n\n    def validate_payee(self, record: Dict[str, Any], line_num: int) -> List[str]:\n        """Validate against payees database."""\n        errors = []\n        \n        account_no = record.get("account_no", "")\n        if not account_no:\n            return errors\n        \n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.payees_db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute("SELECT payee_name, fraud_flag FROM payees WHERE account_no = ?", (account_no,))\n        row = cur.fetchone()\n        conn.close()\n        \n        if not row:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Account {account_no} not found in payee database")\n            return errors\n        \n        db_name, fraud_flag = row\n        \n        if fraud_flag == 1:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Account {account_no} is flagged for fraud risk")\n        \n        file_payee_name = record.get("payee_name", "")\n        if file_payee_name:\n            def normalize_name(name: str) -> str:\n                name = name.strip()\n                name = re.sub(r\'\\s+\', \' \', name)\n                return name.casefold()\n            \n            if normalize_name(file_payee_name) != normalize_name(db_name):\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Payee name mismatch for account {account_no}")\n        \n        return errors\n\n    def validate_file(self, file_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        """Validate an EFT payment file."""\n        content = Path(file_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")\n        file_hash = self._compute_hash(content)\n        is_dup = self.check_duplicate(file_hash)\n        \n        normalized = content.replace("\\r\\n", "\\n").replace("\\r", "\\n")\n        lines = normalized.split("\\n")\n        while lines and lines[-1] == "":\n            lines.pop()\n        \n        records_processed = len(lines)\n        errors: List[str] = []\n        warnings: List[str] = []\n        \n        for i, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):\n            record, rec_errors = self.parse_and_validate_record(line, i)\n            errors.extend(rec_errors)\n        \n        if not is_dup:\n            filename = Path(file_path).name\n            self.record_file(file_hash, filename, records_processed)\n        \n        return {\n            "duplicate": is_dup,\n            "n_errors": len(errors),\n            "n_warnings": len(warnings),\n            "errors": errors,\n            "warnings": warnings,\n            "file_hash": file_hash,\n            "records_processed": records_processed,\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    import argparse\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\'Validate EFT payment files\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--file\', required=True, help=\'Path to EFT file to validate\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--schema\', required=True, help=\'Path to schema JSON\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--index\', required=True, help=\'Path to SQLite index database\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--clearing-accounts\', required=True, help=\'Path to clearing accounts file\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--retention-days\', type=int, default=5, help=\'Duplicate detection retention window (days)\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--payees-db\', help=\'Path to payees database\')\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    try:\n        if not Path(args.file).exists():\n            report = {\n                "duplicate": False,\n                "n_errors": 1,\n                "n_warnings": 0,\n                "errors": [f"File not found: {args.file}"],\n                "warnings": [],\n                "file_hash": "",\n                "records_processed": 0,\n            }\n            print(json.dumps(report))\n            sys.exit(1)\n\n        validator = EFTValidator(\n            schema_path=args.schema,\n            index_db=args.index,\n            clearing_accounts_path=args.clearing_accounts,\n            retention_days=args.retention_days,\n            payees_db_path=args.payees_db\n        )\n\n        report = validator.validate_file(args.file)\n        print(json.dumps(report))\n\n        if report[\'duplicate\'] or report[\'n_errors\'] > 0:\n            sys.exit(1)\n        else:\n            sys.exit(0)\n    \n    except Exception as e:\n        report = {\n            "duplicate": False,\n            "n_errors": 1,\n
